### 2026-11-11 16:49:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Consumer Confidence #Importance 0

Thailand Consumer Morale Weakens in October — The University of the Thai Chamber of Commerce's Consumer Confidence Index declined to 50.9 in October 2026 from 52.1 in September, marking its second consecutive monthly decrease and the lowest reading in four months. The decline was driven by flooding in and around Bangkok, rising energy costs, with retail fuel prices exceeding 40 baht per liter, and concerns over political stability amid reports of a possible no-confidence debate against the government. Sub-indices measuring confidence in the overall economy, job opportunities, and future income also fell for a second straight month, to 40.0, 48.8, and 60.0, respectively. However, consumer spending could receive support toward the end of the year from the government's Thai Chuay Thai Plus stimulus program, the tourism high season, and higher agricultural crop prices.

### 2026-11-11 16:45:36  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

French Stocks Hit Fresh 2-Month High — The CAC 40 rose 0.7% to 8,339 on Wednesday, extending its winning streak to a third consecutive session and reaching its highest level since September. The rally reflected broader strength across global equities, fueled by a surge in technology stocks following CoreWeave’s upbeat outlook, which helped assuage fears surrounding an AI market bubble, as well as growing anticipation for Anthropic PBC’s planned IPO. Sentiment was further buoyed by cooling geopolitical tensions after US Secretary of State Marco Rubio told a G7 gathering in Paris that Washington saw "no need for major combat operations" in Iran. Semiconductor maker STMicroelectronics spearheaded market gains with a 4.1% jump, alongside strong performances from Schneider Electric (2.4%), Legrand (2.1%), and TotalEnergies (1.2%). In contrast, Essilor (-1.8%), ENGIE (-0.2%), and Danone (-0.2%) were among the few decliners.

---

### 2026-11-11 16:43:40  #Trading Economics #Markets #Italy #Stock Market #Importance 0

FTSE MIB Rises for Third Session — Milan’s FTSE MIB rose 0.2% towards 52,000 on Wednesday, extending its advance for a third consecutive session and reaching its highest level since late September, as technology stocks led the gains. Technoprobe surged 6.0%, while STMicroelectronics gained 4.1% and Prysmian advanced 2.7%. The strong performance came amid improved sentiment toward the technology sector, following an upbeat outlook from CoreWeave, which has been at the centre of concerns over a potential AI bubble. Meanwhile, Telecom Italia added 2.3% after reporting third-quarter core earnings broadly in line with expectations. Among other gainers, Saipem rose 1.6%, while Unipol and Azimut Holding both advanced 1.5%. Banking stocks were mixed, with UniCredit up 0.5%, while Intesa Sanpaolo slipped 0.7% and Banco BPM lost 0.4%. On the downside, Nexi fell 0.9%, A2A dropped 0.8% and Banca Mediolanum declined 0.8%.

### 2026-11-11 16:38:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Construction Output #Importance 1

Slovakia Construction Output Falls for Second Consecutive Month — Slovakia’s construction output fell by 4.3% year-on-year in September 2026, marking a second consecutive month of contraction, although the annual decline eased slightly from August’s 4.8% drop, the steepest in nearly two years. Overall domestic construction deteriorated further (-3.6% vs -3.0% in August), reflecting deeper losses in the dominant new construction activity (-5.3% vs -0.7%) and civil engineering works (-19.4% vs -10.3%). In contrast, building construction growth accelerated sharply to 5.6% from 0.6%, while repair and maintenance of buildings rebounded to 1.0% from a 6.4% drop. Construction activity abroad also remained subdued, although its annual contraction moderated to 9.6% from 16.0%. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, construction output increased by 2.2%. In the first nine months of 2026, construction activity was 4.8% higher than in the same period last year.

【摩根士丹利大幅下調三星、海力士長期盈利預期 目標價大降】摩根士丹利與野村對韓國由AI驅動的儲存晶片景氣週期究竟能持續多久觀點分歧巨大。摩根士丹利週一下調SK海力士目標價38%，至160萬韓元（約合1198美元）；同時將三星電子目標價下調19%，至30.9萬韓元。下調理由是：晶片供給增加，疊加2028年資料中心建設進度延後帶來的風險。 野村在上週四釋出的報告中維持原有目標價：SK海力士470萬韓元、三星電子67萬韓元，分別是摩根士丹利調整後目標價的2.9倍與2.2倍。 兩家投行均看好明年盈利表現。摩根士丹利上調三星、SK海力士2027年營業利潤預期，上調幅度分別為9%、13%，但大幅下調了長期利潤率假設。摩根士丹利分析師Shawn Kim在週一報告中表示：“當前儲存晶片市場最核心的爭論，就是我們能否避免2028年出現‘盈利斷崖’。” 對於SK海力士，Kim將其2029–2035年預期平均營業利潤率從53%下調至28%，判斷盈利水平將回歸歷史常態區間。

### 2026-11-11 16:32:15  #宏觀

【國家統計局2026年常規統計督察第2統計督察組向上海市反饋統計督察意見】經黨中央和國務院批准，國家統計局於11月3日向上海市反饋了2026年常規統計督察意見。國家統計局黨組書記、局長康義通報常規統計督察意見，上海市委副書記、市長朱忠明作表態發言。上海市委常委、常務副市長華源主持會議，督察組有關負責同志，上海市有關部門負責同志參加反饋。 督察認為，近年來，上海市堅持以習近平新時代中國特色社會主義思想為指導，深入學習貫徹習近平總書記關於統計工作重要講話和指示批示精神，紮實開展統計普查調查工作，著力加強統計法治建設，積極推動統計現代化改革創新，以可靠統計保障有力推動上海加快建成具有世界影響力的社會主義現代化國際大都市。 督察發現，上海市統計工作仍然存在一些問題，主要表現在：一是有的區和部門學習貫徹黨中央和國務院關於防治統計造假重大決策部署有差距。二是有的區和部門落實防治統計造假責任還有欠缺。三是統計法定職責履行仍有短板。四是實地調查地區部分企業資料失實。五是統計基層基礎建設抓得不實。六是首輪統計督察整改落實有差距，整改監督和成果運用不足。

### 2026-11-11 16:29:20  #MKT News #Brent #Impact bullish #WTI

【European gas rally continues; futures top €85/MWh】European benchmark gas futures climbed above €85/MWh after a 2.7% rise the prior session as traders assess Middle East conflict risks and regional supply uncertainty. Talks to end the Iran war have made little progress and attacks on tankers near the Strait of Hormuz continued this week. IRNA reported Iran’s top security official denied negotiating with the US and called US claims of Qatar-mediated talks false and intended to depress oil prices. European LNG imports lag year-earlier levels and abnormally low winter stocks are drawing down with the heating season. This winter’s supply balance for Europe hinges on a rapid recovery of Strait of Hormuz shipments, especially Qatari flows. The Iran conflict has removed roughly one-fifth of global LNG exports from the market, forcing Europe to compete with Asia for scarce cargoes; regional gas prices are at their highest since 2022 and more than three times their level at the start of the year.

FTSE 100 Rebounds on Wednesday — The FTSE 100 rose 0.8% on Wednesday, recovering from the previous session’s 0.3% decline, as gains in commodity-linked stocks and banks supported the index. Oil majors Shell and BP advanced 1% and 1.6%, respectively, as crude prices attempted to rebound following a two-day selloff, with investors continuing to assess prospects for an end to the US-Iran conflict. Mining stocks also strengthened as metals recovered from the previous session’s losses. Antofagasta climbed nearly 4%, while Anglo American, Glencore and Rio Tinto gained at least 2%, supported by rebounds in copper, gold and silver. Banks also contributed to the advance, with HSBC, Lloyds Banking Group and Barclays each rising by 1% or more. Elsewhere, Spirax-Sarco Engineering gained 2% after the company expressed confidence in meeting its full-year targets, citing improved sales growth at its Steam Thermal Solutions division in the four months to the end of October.

【韓資管大佬提議：半導體暴利稅建設韓股託底機制 為新生代配置資產】韓國股市波動處於極端水平之際，韓國投資管理公司（Korea Investment Management）執行長Bae Jae-kyu提議，將半導體景氣帶來的暴利稅收入，用於搭建股市安全墊，併為年輕人積累資產。他的方案並非一次性財政支出或短期市場平準基金，而是設立一項具備自我造血能力的長效基金：國家按市值權重長期買入股票，並將股份分配給下一代群體，包括新生兒。Bae Jae-kyu建議，晶片龍頭等出口企業盈利大增帶來的這筆額外暴利稅收入，不投向消費類財政專案，而是做長期配置，分散投資於韓國本土上市股票以及摩根士丹利資本國際指數（MSCI）等全球資產。方案核心是政府按照市值權重，程式化、持續買入主機板與科斯達克（KOSDAQ）上市標的，充當穩定的市場託底力量，再將累積持倉劃轉至下一代。

【SpaceX據悉嘗試機架層“去英偉達化”：只買晶片 研發Kessel機櫃繞過L6計算板】半導體研究機構SemiAnalysis在X上披露，SpaceX正在做一件此前只有超大規模雲廠商嘗試過的事：保留英偉達的晶片，但把晶片周圍的機架自己重做一遍。這個自研機架的內部代號是Kessel。 SemiAnalysis稱，SpaceX計劃直接向英偉達採購晶片封裝，其餘部件自行採購，從而跳過英偉達的L6計算板。該機構寫道：“這也改變了誰買哪些零件：SpaceX計劃向英偉達購買晶片封裝、其餘直接採購，繞過英偉達的L6計算板。這會讓SpaceX在供應商選擇與零部件採購上擁有更多控制權。”跳過L6，意味著SpaceX拿到了板卡這一層的設計權與採購權——PCB、聯結器、供電、散熱這些環節，從“英偉達指定”變成“SpaceX自選”。

【India bans exports of lithium‑ion battery black mass】India on Tuesday imposed an immediate export ban on lithium‑ion battery black mass — a recycling material containing lithium, cobalt, nickel and manganese — reclassifying it from free export to prohibited. Notifications from the Ministry of Commerce and Industry and the Department of Commerce say the ban covers lithium‑ion battery black mass and unclassified battery waste not containing lead, cadmium or mercury, and aims to encourage domestic processing of critical battery materials to support India’s EV supply‑chain strategy. ### 2026-11-11 15:57:38  #公司

【興業證券吳峰：市場步入情緒修復的反彈週期】興業證券市場策略首席分析師吳峰日前接受證券時報記者採訪時表示，隨著A股三季報披露收官，疊加10月末、11月初國內外一系列宏觀事件“靴子落地”，11月以來A股告別前期受不確定性壓制的低風險偏好階段，步入情緒修復的反彈週期。本輪修復更多源於估值與風險偏好的回擺，事件落地、預期明朗，是行情由築底轉向修復的核心催化。中期維度看，投融資關係的制度平衡正在深刻重塑A股市場生態，構築市場堅實的制度底盤。上市公司回報端持續最佳化，9月以來產業資本掀起新一輪迴購增持浪潮，一批現金流穩健的行業龍頭推出大額回購方案，登出式回購佔比抬升，直接增厚每股收益，其可持續性以及對每股收益的增厚效應，顯著強於過往象徵性回購。更為關鍵的是，2024年以來全部A股稅前分紅與回購總額持續高於股權融資總額及產業資本淨減持規模之和，扭轉了A股過往淨融資的格局。與此同時，減持新規將股東減持與破發、破淨、分紅達標情況硬性掛鉤，約束無序套現行為；規則覆蓋違規立案處罰期、短線交易、協議轉讓以及離婚、分立等非交易過戶場景，繞道減持的空間顯著收窄。資本市場穩市機制持續完善，匯金類“平準基金”定位、互換便利、增持回購再貸款等工具構建長效支撐體系，有望增強股市內在穩定性，推動資本市場平穩健康發展。吳峰表示，股權融資、股東回報、股東減持、穩市機制等維度共同形成完整制度閉環，能夠有效託底市場執行的底部區間。

### 2026-11-11 15:56:52  #公司 #市場

【羅兵鹹永道資本市場合夥人李慧婷：2027年科技企業仍將是港股IPO主力】近日，在第九屆中國國際進口博覽會期間，羅兵鹹永道資本市場合夥人李慧婷分析當前港股IPO市場結構變化，表示科技企業已經成為港股IPO的主力軍，這一趨勢在2027年仍將得到延續。她表示，這背後有兩個原因。第一，中國科技產業進入新一輪資本市場週期。科技企業已從傳統網際網路平臺延伸至人工智慧、半導體、機器人、智慧駕駛、新能源、低空經濟、太空科技等領域。科技企業研發投入高、技術迭代快，推進商業化與國際化時需要大量長期資本。IPO不僅是融資工具，更是支撐企業進入下一增長階段的重要平臺，境外上市仍是科技公司的戰略選項。第二，香港上市制度越來越能承接新經濟企業。港交所18C制度為特專科技公司提供上市渠道，涵蓋新一代資訊科技、先進硬體及軟體、新能源等領域。香港已形成全面支援科技企業上市的生態。對仍處高研發投入階段、未必符合傳統盈利標準的企業，香港市場的包容性非常重要。香港IPO市場正由傳統融資中心進一步吸納更多科技創新企業，未來預計有更多科技企業選擇來港上市。

【CPO、PCB等集體爆發，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）放量大漲，連續兩日獲淨申購】11月11日，算力硬體概念爆發，CPO、PCB方向領漲，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）放量大漲5.7%，成交額4.22億元，資金流向方面，已連續兩日獲得淨申購，最新規模9.3億元。訊息面上，光模組政策緩和跡象出現，資本開支維持強勢。方正證券指出，海外加快算力星座部署，將壓縮軌道、頻率等資源空間，倒逼國內加快算力星座規劃發射，同時國內多項太空算力計劃陸續公佈，海內外行業進展共振，太空算力將進入高速發展階段。長城證券認為，算力裝置功耗和部署密度提升，疊加資料中心能效要求趨嚴，有望共同推動液冷應用擴大。隨著介面標準逐步完善和專案交付經驗積累，液冷產業鏈的競爭優勢有望更多體現在產品可靠性、系統適配能力和工程交付效率。建議關注冷板、CDU、快速接頭等關鍵環節，以及具備全鏈條產品佈局、系統適配能力和專案交付經驗的廠商。華龍證券表示，AI產業帶動下，行業需求明顯修復，業績持續邊際改善，復甦趨勢進一步鞏固。政策指引疊加國產替代推進將持續提振科技內需，建議關注計算機裝置行業中的AI基建相關環節，包括AI伺服器、算力服務及散熱。資料顯示，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）緊密跟蹤創業板算力基礎設施指數，指數聚焦AI算力基礎設施產業鏈，是一鍵佈局算力硬體核心環節的便捷工具。

【NIESR says Bank of England should raise policy rate to about 5%】The National Institute of Economic and Social Research said on Wednesday the Bank of England would need to raise its policy rate to roughly 5% to successfully curb inflation within three years. Last week the BoE raised its rate from 3.75% to 4%—its first hike in over three years—and signalled a possible further rise while opposing market pricing of a 5% terminal rate. NIESR said investors pricing larger hikes were justified and policymakers may be moving too slowly. It forecasts inflation will peak at 4.4% YoY in February; if the BoE raises to 4.25% then holds, inflation would not return to the 2% target until 2030.

Azerbaijan GDP Grows 1.7% in January–October — Azerbaijan’s economy grew 1.7% year-on-year in January–October 2026, following a 1.5% expansion in the first nine months of the year. The non-oil and gas sector continued to advance (2.9% vs 2.6% in January–September), while contraction in the oil and gas sector eased (-0.9% vs -1.2%). Growth accelerated in transportation and warehousing services (10.2% vs 10.0%) and agricultural output (1.4% vs 1.1%). Industrial output also declined at a slower pace (-0.7% vs -0.8%). Meanwhile, growth moderated in information and communication services (9.9% vs 11.1%), while retail trade turnover growth remained unchanged at 3.8%. Investment in fixed capital growth slowed to 9.7% from 10.9% previously, amid weaker growth in the non-oil and gas sector (2.3% vs 3.1%).

### 2026-11-11 15:41:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

India 10Y Yield Extends Rebound — India’s 10-year government bond yield rose to around 7.20%, extending its rebound after hitting an over-one-month low, as higher US Treasury yields and concerns over additional bond supply weighed on debt prices. The US 10-year Treasury yield jumped toward 5% following stronger-than-expected inflation, reducing the relative attractiveness of Indian debt and potentially limiting foreign investment. At the same time, substantial government borrowing needs and continued issuance of the benchmark 10-year bond add to securities supply, while tighter banking-system liquidity could constrain domestic demand. Persistent domestic inflation may also limit the Reserve Bank of India’s scope for rate cuts, keeping borrowing costs elevated. However, crude oil prices fell 17% last week, easing imported inflation risks and pressure on India’s external balance and government finances, potentially giving the RBI greater room to ease monetary policy and putting downward pressure on yields.

【Ukraine's path to EU membership narrows as war entrenches resistance】Refinitiv interviews with more than a dozen EU diplomats and officials show growing private caution about Ukraine's EU accession, saying both Kyiv and the EU must overcome substantial hurdles. Gerald Knaus, chair of the European Stability Initiative, said: In many countries, politicians do not expect Ukraine to join the EU within the next 10 years. A diplomat added that a wave of national elections across Europe next year could leave governments with little bandwidth to campaign for enlargement.

### 2026-11-11 15:26:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lithuania #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Lithuania Producer Inflation Picks Up in October — Producer prices in Lithuania jumped 18.2% year-on-year in October 2026 from an upwardly revised three-year high of 17.7% in the previous month. Price growth accelerated in manufacturing (18.5% vs 17.5% in September), driven largely by stronger gains in the production of coke and refined petroleum products (88.1% vs 85.3%). Moreover, costs increased in mining and quarrying (8.6% vs 6.0%). On the other hand, costs grew at a softer pace in electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply (14.5% vs 18.1%) and water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation activities (4.7% vs 7.0%). On a monthly basis, producer inflation eased sharply to 0.4% in October 2026, following an upwardly revised 3.7% in the preceding period.

China Total Vehicle Sales Fall 9.2% in October — China's total vehicle sales dropped 9.2% year-on-year to 3.016 million units in October 2026, following an 8.4% decline in September, according to the China Association of Automobile Manufacturers (CAAM). New energy vehicle (NEV) sales remained a bright spot, rising 13.1% to a record high of 1.94 million units, although growth eased from 13.6% in September, as strong export growth offset a decline in domestic sales. NEVs accounted for 64.3% of total sales, up from 61.6% in September, marking the fourth straight month above 60%. However, total vehicle sales increased 2% month-on-month in October.

### 2026-11-11 15:19:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Turkey Retail Sales Hit Over 2-Year Low — Retail sales in Turkey increased by 7.7% year-on-year in September 2026, easing from a downwardly revised 10% in the previous month. It marked the lowest reading since July 2024, reflecting slower sales growth in automotive fuel in specialized stores (6% vs 6.8% in August), cultural and recreation goods in specialized stores (7.2% vs 7.9%), other goods in specialized stores (9.9% vs 20.5%), sales via stalls and markets (3.8% vs 10.7%), and trade not in stores, stalls or markets (17.1% vs 17.9%). Meanwhile, sales growth accelerated in non-specialized stores (6.6% vs 5.6%), food, beverages and tobacco in specialized stores (4% vs 2.9%), and information and communication equipment in specialized stores (12.9% vs 8.6%). In addition, sales of other household equipment in specialized stores declined at a softer pace (-1.3% vs -2%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail sales slowed sharply to 0.1% from an upwardly revised and three-month high of 1.6% in August.

### 2026-11-11 15:18:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Interest Rate #Importance 1

BoT Backs Accommodative Policy Amid Uneven Recovery: Oct Minutes — Thailand's monetary policy should remain accommodative to support an economic recovery that remains weak and uneven, with growth below potential, according to the October policy meeting minutes. The Committee emphasized that monetary policy primarily manages aggregate demand and economic stability, while fiscal and targeted government measures are needed to address structural challenges, inequality, limited SME financing, and weak competitiveness. Members also cautioned that maintaining low interest rates amid global monetary policy divergence could heighten financial stability risks through capital flows, exchange rate volatility, and domestic investor behavior, although Thailand's external stability remained strong with no significant signs of vulnerability. The committee voted to keep the policy rate unchanged, noting that oil prices and El Niño could intensify inflationary pressures and cost pass-through, warranting close monitoring.

### 2026-11-11 15:18:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Household Spending MoM #Importance 1

Sweden Household Spending Accelerates in October — Household consumption in Sweden rose 1.2% month-on-month in October 2026, accelerating from 0.5% in September. Spending increased most sharply on recreation, sports and culture (6.4% vs 0.9% in September), clothing and footwear (5.6% vs 1.8%), furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (5.3% vs 2.2%), and restaurants and accommodation services (5.1% vs -0.1%). Consumption also strengthened in personal care, social protection and miscellaneous goods and services (3.1% vs 1.5%). Meanwhile, spending continued to decline on housing, water, electricity, gas and other fuels (-1.9% vs -0.5%), healthcare (-1.1% vs 0.4%), transportation (-2.1% vs 0.9%), and information and communication (-2.4% vs 0.4%). Growth in food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.6% vs 2.3%) and alcoholic beverages and tobacco (1.1% vs 3.5%) moderated. On a yearly basis, household consumption increased 3.3% in October, up from 2.7% in September.

### 2026-11-11 15:16:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Germany October Inflation Confirmed at 2.8% — Germany’s annual inflation rate eased to 2.8% in October 2026, down from a near three-year high of 3.3% in September, confirming preliminary estimates. The slowdown was attributed to the new fuel discount introduced at the beginning of October, which is set to remain in place until year-end. Energy inflation moderated to 12.2% from 14.9% in September, as price growth slowed for motor fuels (28.9% vs 37.1%), although heating oil inflation accelerated (84.5% vs 74.9%). Meanwhile, food inflation was unchanged at 0.4%, as higher prices for eggs (15.4%), fresh vegetables, including potatoes (11.5%), and fish, fish products and seafood (4.5%) were partly offset by lower prices for edible fats and oils (-9.1%, including butter: -18.9%), dairy products (-4.7%) and fresh fruit (-2.8%). Core inflation, which excludes energy and unprocessed food, eased to 2.1% from 2.4%. On a monthly basis, the CPI decreased by 0.2%, reversing a 0.6% rise in September.

【外交部：中方期待推動《中國—東盟自貿區3.0版升級議定書》儘快生效】11月11日，外交部發言人郭嘉昆主持例行記者會。總檯記者提問：中方剛剛釋出了李強總理出席中國—東盟領導人會議的訊息。中方對此次會議有何期待？郭嘉昆表示，今年是中國—東盟建立全面戰略伙伴關係5週年。5年來，在雙方領導人的共同引領下，中國東盟合作蓬勃發展、成果豐碩，始終走在地區前列，不僅惠及各自國家和人民，也為地區和平穩定繁榮作出重要貢獻。當前，世界進入新的動盪變革期，求和平、謀發展、促繁榮是地區人民的最大期盼。中方期待透過此次與會，與東盟國家增進團結互信，凝聚合作共識，推動《中國—東盟自貿區3.0版升級議定書》儘快生效，深化人工智慧、數字經濟、能源安全、互聯互通、減貧發展等領域合作，攜手建設和平、安寧、繁榮、美麗、友好的共同家園，為地區的和平發展注入更多的穩定性和確定性。

### 2026-11-11 15:11:31  #MKT News #Market Regions #Emerging Markets

【South Korea fines 15 firms $1.21bn for collusion in government bond auctions】The Korea Fair Trade Commission (KFTC) fined 15 domestic financial firms a total of 1.627 trillion won (about $1.21bn) for colluding in Korean government bond auctions. The KFTC said a year-long probe found primary dealers — five banks and ten securities firms — shared information or coordinated bid yields and sizes in 145 auctions from Jan 2020 to Jun 2023, artificially suppressing yields and harming the treasury. Seven firms will be referred to prosecutors for criminal charges for longer or more frequent involvement; KB Securities, NH Investment & Securities and Mirae Asset Securities are among those facing prosecution and drew some of the largest fines.

Japanese Stocks Extend Gains for Third Consecutive Session — Japan’s Nikkei 225 Index advanced 1.31% to close at 61,249 on Wednesday, recording its third straight session of gains as stronger-than-expected results from AI computing provider CoreWeave eased concerns over the artificial intelligence sector. Optimism surrounding AI-related stocks was further boosted by reports that Anthropic could begin trading as early as next week, with plans to raise up to $90 billion at a diluted valuation of approximately $1.4 trillion. Technology and AI-related stocks led the gains, including Kioxia Holdings (5.6%), Advantest (5.9%), Taiyo Yuden (7.3%) and Lasertec (6.3%). Meanwhile, shares of SoftBank Group fell after posting its first net loss in nearly two years, driven by a $3.7 billion impairment charge on its US data-center projects and declining valuations across the AI sector.

【中國網際網路金融協會網際網路貸款自律工作委員會籌備組第三次會議在京召開】據中國網際網路金融協會訊息，11月6日，中國網際網路金融協會網際網路貸款自律工作委員會籌備組第三次會議在京召開。協會秘書處主持會議，度小滿、螞蟻科技、騰訊、京東科技、抖音等籌備組成員單位出席會議。會議審議透過籌備工作報告，同意按流程提請成立工作委員會。會議認為，籌備組自成立以來，重點聚焦網際網路助貸業務領域，密切關注行業發展動態，深入推動政策貫徹落實，研究報送多期熱點前沿問題情況專報，組織開展信貸資金挪用風險專項審計，以高標準、嚴要求的實際行動引領助貸平臺規範健康發展。會議強調，工作委員會是組織實施助貸平臺自律管理的機制保障。成員單位應當具備強烈的合規意願和較高的合規水平，要嚴把准入關，按照更高標準、更嚴要求穩慎推進成員單位遴選工作；同時，透過資訊報送、自律檢查、專項審計、年度評估評價等方式，加強對成員單位的自律管理。

### 2026-11-11 14:56:27  #MKT News #Important #Market Themes #AI Revolution #CSI300 #Impact mixed #Hang Seng Index

【UBS strategist: foreign investors' China equity allocations unchanged; still constructive on China AI tech hardware】UBS China equity strategist Wang Zonghao said UBS’s tracking of about 800 active foreign funds shows foreign institutional allocations to China equities were broadly unchanged in Q3. By sector, international investors net increased positions most in healthcare, internet and technology—likely aided by IPOs and placements—and trimmed insurance, basic materials and autos. UBS remains constructive on China AI tech hardware given attractive risk/reward, but warns the recent global tech sell-off could produce further spillovers into domestic names. With AI sentiment still fragile and public mutual funds’ AI hardware holdings remaining elevated, Wang recommends a barbell approach pairing tech hardware with banks.

【瑞銀王宗豪：外資中國股票倉位總體不變，仍看好中國AI科技硬體】瑞銀中國股票策略研究主管王宗豪指出，瑞銀對約800只外資主動型基金的跟蹤顯示，三季度外資機構投資者的中國股票倉位總體保持不變。分行業看，國際投資者增持最多的是醫療保健、網際網路和科技板塊（可能來自IPO和配售），同時減持了保險、基礎材料和汽車板塊。鑑於風險回報具有吸引力，瑞銀仍看好中國AI科技硬體，但近期全球科技股大跌可能對中國國內相關個股產生進一步連鎖影響。鑑於AI的市場情緒仍較脆弱，且公募基金的AI科技硬體持倉仍處於高位，他建議採用科技硬體與銀行相結合的槓鈴策略。

【華福證券：維持安克創新“買入”評級，主業盈利能力持續改善】華福證券研報指出，安克創新主業盈利能力持續改善，渠道結構最佳化與新品類拓展驅動長期成長。2026Q3實現營業收入95.35億元，同比增長16.96%；歸母淨利潤5.59億元，同比下降27.04%。公司收入端受到短期銷售節奏影響，但扣非利潤增幅明顯高於收入增幅，體現主營業務的盈利能力有所增強。毛利率改善與費用投放最佳化形成合力，持續研發投入鞏固技術優勢。自有銷售體系加速成長，線下渠道成為收入增長的重要貢獻來源，家庭能源與智慧硬體同步推進，產品矩陣向高價值場景延伸。考慮到今年公司收到退回的部分上年度繳納的IEEPA關稅，相應地帶來營業成本的減少，上調2026年盈利預測，維持“買入”評級。

### 2026-11-11 14:52:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Steel Holds Steady Amid Anticipated Production Cuts — Steel rebar futures traded near CNY 3,040 per ton, holding relatively steady as markets weighed the prospect of production cuts across major steelmaking hubs in China’s Hebei province. Key steel-producing cities, including Tangshan and Handan, have entered the autumn-winter air pollution control period, during which unfavorable weather conditions have periodically disrupted sintering operations and blast furnace utilization. These potential restrictions have added pressure to steel prices, which were already facing seasonal weakness in demand. Over the longer term, the World Steel Association revised its outlook, projecting global steel demand to contract by 0.1% this year, compared with its previous forecast of 0.3% growth. The association also expects China's steel consumption to decline by 2% in 2026, as the downturn in the property sector continues to persist longer than previously anticipated.

### 2026-11-11 14:48:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Iron Ore Slides on Expected Steel Production Cuts — Iron ore futures slipped toward CNY 680 per ton on Wednesday, reaching a one-week low as expectations of production restrictions across major steelmaking hubs in China’s Hebei province weighed on demand prospects. Key steel-producing cities, including Tangshan and Handan, have entered the autumn-winter air pollution control period, during which unfavorable weather conditions have periodically disrupted sintering operations and blast furnace utilization. Expectations are growing that broader production restrictions could be introduced across Hebei, potentially placing further pressure on steel output. Industry data also showed that average hot metal production among Chinese steelmakers, a key indicator of iron ore demand, continued to decline. Meanwhile, global iron ore shipments decreased by 5.57 million tons to 29.91 million tons in the week ending November 8. Shipments from Australia and Brazil also fell by 3.84 million tons to 24.06 million tons.

【Analysis: Anthropic lock-up expiry could trigger AI sector drawdown】Jack Selby, head of venture firm Copper Sky, warned Anthropic’s large, high-profile IPO may mark the peak of AI investment and that expiry of the initial lock-up could spark a chain sell-off in the AI sector. He said a disappointing IPO showing would likely be a key catalyst for a broad AI market correction and questioned claims of “unlimited” AI demand. Selby added that, if conditions permit, he would take large long positions in China AI, arguing Anthropic cannot compel all customers to use U.S.-based models and customer migration to non-U.S. models could create arbitrage opportunities.

Rupiah Weakens as Dollar Firms and Political Risks Mount — The Indonesian rupiah slipped past IDR 18,150 per U.S. dollar on Wednesday, reversing recent gains as a firmer dollar weighed on sentiment following hotter-than-expected U.S. inflation that fueled bets on further Fed rate hikes. The rupiah led regional losses amid mounting political uncertainty after Vice President Gibran Rakabuming reportedly offered to resign amid legal challenges over the validity of his education documents. Concerns over foreign outflows also resurfaced ahead of MSCI's November review of Indonesia's stock market. Meanwhile, recent retail sales data pointed to cooling consumer spending despite robust Q3 GDP growth. Bank Indonesia continued intervening to support the currency, but concerns grew over a policy dilemma between defending the rupiah and sustaining economic growth. The central bank will hold its November policy meeting next week after maintaining its key rate for a fourth straight month in October, following cumulative hikes of 100bps in May and June.

### 2026-11-11 14:13:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Machine Tool Orders #Importance 1

Japan Machine Tool Orders Rise 42.5% in October — Japan’s machine tool orders increased by 42.5% year-on-year to JPY 204,377 million in October 2026, easing from a 60.4% surge in the previous month. Demand remained robust across both domestic and overseas markets, with domestic orders rising 46.5% year-on-year to JPY 52,290 million, despite falling 18.2% from September, while foreign orders climbed 41.1% to JPY 152,087 million, recording a smaller monthly decline of 4.5%. Overseas demand continued to account for the bulk of total orders, representing nearly 75% of the October figure. On a monthly basis, total orders declined by 8.4%, slowing from a 12.8% rise in the preceding period. In the first ten months of 2026, cumulative machine tool orders increased 43.2% year-on-year to JPY 1,873,680 million, supported by gains of 46.8% in foreign orders and 33.9% in domestic demand.

【會計改革與發展“十五五”規劃：推動財務造假存量加快出清、增量得到有效遏制】財政部印發《會計改革與發展“十五五”規劃》。其中提出，會計監督作用更好發揮。會計監督體系更加健全，打擊財務造假工作機制進一步完善，對重點領域的監督、突出問題的整治持續發力，精準性、有效性不斷提升，推動財務造假存量加快出清、增量得到有效遏制。嚴格落實會計法等法律法規，壓實各類主體責任，建立權責清晰的責任體系。聚焦國計民生重要行業和關鍵領域持續開展會計資訊質量檢查，以“清存量、遏增量”為目標，嚴肅查處財務造假行為。嚴厲打擊虛構經濟業務、虛增利潤粉飾報表，編制虛假財務會計報告等違法違規行為，深挖造假鏈條，依法查處第三方配合造假行為，加快出清造假存量。堅持關口前移、防患未然，健全常態化監測預警機制，運用大資料、資訊化手段提升監管能力，源頭遏制造假增量。以查處嚴重違法行為和性質惡劣案件為抓手，對涉案單位和責任人依法追責問責。強化執法資訊公開和新聞宣傳，暢通群眾監督、輿論監督渠道，釋出會計資訊質量檢查公告和典型案例，持續發揮“以案釋法”作用，推動單位有效執行國家統一的會計制度，健全完善內部會計監督機制和內部控制體系。

【我國新能源汽車銷量佔比持續走高】中國汽車工業協會今天（11日）釋出的最新資料顯示，10月份，我國汽車產銷量雙雙超過300萬輛，比上月呈現一定幅度的增長，其中，新能源汽車市場銷量佔比持續走高。最新資料顯示，10月份，我國新能源汽車產銷量分別完成201.7萬輛和194萬輛，同比分別增長13.8%和13.1%，新能源汽車新車銷量達到汽車新車總銷量的64.3%。今年以來，我國新能源汽車市場主體地位不斷鞏固，累計銷量佔比持續提升，1至10月達到54.8%。以新能源乘用車為例，10月份國內市場佔比更是超過了七成。專家表示，我國新能源汽車銷量佔比持續走高，不是短期現象，是產業技術迭代、產品力提升、市場接受度提高共同作用的結果。中國汽車工業協會的最新資料同時顯示，我國汽車出口量已連續五個月超過100萬輛，新能源汽車出口佔比連續五個月超過50%。其中10月份，新能源汽車出口60.6萬輛，同比增長1.4倍。

ASX 200 Closes Higher as Markets Recover from Sluggish Start — The ASX 200 added 24 points, or 0.3%, to finish at 9,153 on Wednesday, extending gains for a third session while hovering near an 11-week high. The index rebounded from a sluggish start, helped by stronger U.S. stock futures after Wall Street losses Tuesday following hot U.S. inflation data that reinforced bets for a December Fed rate hike. Sentiment also drew support from China, Australia's largest trading partner, where the trade surplus surpassed USD 1 trillion in the first 10 months of 2026 for a second straight year, buoyed by strong demand for AI-related products despite weak domestic consumption. Markets looked past Australia's weak Q3 lending indicator, which reflected the impact of elevated borrowing costs. Industrial services, manufacturing, and utilities led gains, offsetting weakness in commercial services and consumer durables. BHP Group (1.0%) and Rio Tinto (0.4%) reversed early losses. Tech stocks also advanced, led by WiseTech Global (3.0%) and Xero Ltd. (2.0%).

### 2026-11-11 13:38:46  #公司 #市場 #焦點

【儲存又在醞釀新一輪漲價】儲存市場的新一輪漲價，正以一種不太對稱的方式展開。近日，多位儲存行業人士向記者透露，部分頭部企業已向渠道商口頭告知企業級顆粒的漲價意向，提價函尚未正式下發，但渠道端的價格已經先動了起來。記者走訪代理渠道，多位代理商證實了上述漲價訊號。海力士代理商袁先生告訴記者：“每天都在漲”。他觀察到，二手盤迴收價格一直在往上走，並帶動整體報價上行；價格調整的路徑則是省代每週調價，代理產品價格再往下傳，“我們跟著調”。不過，三星代理商李明（化名）提醒記者，這輪漲價並不是全品類普漲：漲價主要集中在DRAM合約端和企業級SSD原廠/渠道端，並非消費級NAND的全面跟漲。“大頭還是算力中心，基層反饋的需求其實一般。”由於漲價先集中在DRAM合約端和企業級SSD環節，這輪價格從原廠往外傳時，首先抵達的不是終端消費者，而是夾在中間的製造企業和模組廠商。李明補充說，這些廠商是原廠漲價的第一道承接方，在產業鏈中的位置不同，感受和壓力也明顯不同。 (藍鯨新聞)

【法國專家報告建議對生成式人工智慧加強監管】法國總統府10日釋出公報說，法國一個專家委員會當天向總統馬克龍提交報告，建議對生成式人工智慧加強監管，透過限制奉承迎合機制、開展上市前跨學科風險評估、加強教育等措施，防範生成式人工智慧對個人認知能力、心理健康及社會執行帶來的潛在危害。這份報告由法國人工智慧與數字委員會組織編寫。9名跨學科專家在開展55場聽證後，圍繞個人和社會層面的風險提出18項政策建議。報告指出，生成式人工智慧正得到廣泛使用，今年年初法國已有3300萬獨立使用者，但四分之一的法國人對其潛在風險表示擔憂。

NZX 50 Halts Decline, Ends at Record High — The NZX 50 rose 12 points, or 0.1%, to close at a new record high of 14,180 on Wednesday, halting losses from the morning session and extending gains after touching a record high a day earlier, mainly buoyed by gains in the tech and financial sectors. Easing oil prices lifted sentiment as inflation concerns and expectations of interest rate hikes eased. However, traders remained cautious, awaiting the announcement of final election results in November 2027 while monitoring coalition talks. Meanwhile, Labour market leaders identified National's Simeon Brown and Labour's Barbara Edmonds as the leading candidates for Finance Minister. The names emerged after current Finance Minister Nicola Willis failed to secure a seat in Parliament. A downbeat session on Wall Street overnight also capped gains as inflation came in hotter than expected. Among the top performers were Gentrack Group (9.1%), Sanford (3.6%), Meridian Energy (2.8%), Ventia Services (2.6%), and Scales Corporation (2.0%).

【農業銀行A股股價5年累計漲幅居銀行業首位】2021年初至2026年11月10日收盤，農業銀行A股股價累計漲幅231%，居銀行業首位，年化收益率達24%；H股股價累計漲幅284%，居國有大行首位，年化收益率26%。其中，2023-2025年，農行A股、H股股價累計漲幅分別為215%、173%，總市值漲幅連續三年居四大行首位， 2025年一度實現境內上市公司A股市值、總市值第一。 
從總市值表現看，2021年初農行總市值1.08萬億元，與當時境內銀行總市值最高的工行相比， 前者僅為後者的63%。到2025年， 農行總市值一度超過工行， 成為境內上市銀行總市值第一。2026年以來， 農行總市值有所回撥，但從7月開始重拾升勢。目前農行總市值2.53萬億， 約為工行的90%， 市淨率0.9倍， 為國有大行最高。（金融一線）

Korean Won Rises on Equity Inflows and Export Growth — The South Korean won strengthened to around 1,336 per dollar, reaching its highest level since late October, as robust export growth and easing foreign selling pressure supported the currency. Strong domestic data also lifted sentiment, with exports more than doubling year-on-year in the first 10 days of November, driven by a 331.2% surge in semiconductor shipments, while employment rose by 139,000 in October, marking the fourth consecutive month of job growth. Foreign investors also returned as net buyers of Korean equities in the previous session, providing additional support. Hopes for a US-Iran diplomatic resolution improved risk appetite, while expectations of fewer US military strikes eased concerns over prolonged Middle Eastern oil supply disruptions. Copper Rebounds on Tightening Supply in China — Copper futures climbed toward $6.30 per pound on Wednesday, recovering from four-month lows amid tightening physical supply in China, the world’s largest copper consumer. Import premiums in the country have reached record levels in recent weeks, while refined copper shipments have declined this year and domestic smelters have reduced production due to rising ore costs and weaker profit margins. China has also faced difficulties replenishing its inventories as substantial volumes of copper have been redirected to the US ahead of potential import tariffs. Additional support came from renewed investor interest in the artificial intelligence sector following strong earnings from neocloud provider CoreWeave. Copper benefits directly from the global expansion of AI infrastructure due to its essential role in data centers and power networks. However, a Democratic sweep in the US midterm elections threatened tighter AI regulations and slower data center development.

Soybeans Steady After Sharp Rally — Soybean futures held above $12.1 per bushel, after climbing 3.4% in the previous session to rebound from multi-month lows, as the US Department of Agriculture (USDA) tightened its US supply outlook by cutting production and inventory forecasts more than expected. The agency’s US soybean production estimate came in 23 million bushels below the average analyst forecast, while its projection for domestic and export demand prompted a 15-million-bushel reduction in expected end-of-season stocks, against market expectations for an increase. Meanwhile, the USDA maintained its South American soybean production outlook. However, gains were partly tempered by weaker demand signals from China, where October soybean imports fell to their lowest monthly level since 2023 amid high crusher inventories, negative margins and low pork prices. Japan 10-Year Yield Tracks US Treasury Yields Higher — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield rose to around 2.77% on Wednesday, reaching a one-week high as it followed the upward movement in US Treasury yields. Markets are now pricing in roughly a 91% probability of a Fed rate increase next month, up from 84% a day earlier, with additional hikes in January and potentially April also being considered. Meanwhile, the Bank of Japan’s latest Summary of Opinions showed broad agreement among policymakers that interest rates should continue rising, as underlying inflation has reached levels generally consistent with the central bank’s 2% price stability target. Platinum Trades Near 3-Week Highs — Platinum futures traded around $1,560 per ounce, near three-week highs as traders weighed easing geopolitical concerns against a subdued demand outlook and rising US interest-rate expectations. A Democratic sweep in the US midterm elections could complicate President Donald Trump's foreign policy agenda and efforts to secure additional funding for the war with Iran, raising hopes of constraints on further military escalation and easing energy-driven inflation concerns. However, hotter-than-expected US inflation has increased bets on a Federal Reserve rate hike next month, limiting demand for the non-yielding metal. Elevated financing costs, weaker vehicle production and the gradual shift toward EVs are also weighing on automotive demand, a major source of platinum consumption. ### 2026-11-11 10:10:58  #其他

【我國科研人員破解玉米鎳元素“運輸密碼”】鎳元素在植物中如何被運輸、對作物產量具體有何影響，一直是困擾科學家的謎題。我國科研人員找到了答案。 中國科學院分子植物科學卓越創新中心、上海張江智種中心晁代印研究團隊聯合華中農業大學嚴建兵教授團隊，透過比較1404份玉米材料的基因差異和籽粒鎳含量，首次鑑定出控制禾本科作物籽粒鎳積累的關鍵轉運蛋白ZmNAET1，揭示了鎳在玉米中的運輸機制，並展現了鎳對於作物產量和種子萌發的影響。相關成果11日在國際學術期刊《細胞》線上發表。 據研究團隊介紹，玉米要把鎳從莖節中送進籽粒和生長點，需要一套精巧的運輸系統，而ZmNAET1就是這套運輸系統的關鍵“出口”，負責把植物體內一種叫煙胺的小分子送到細胞外，而煙胺再像小夾子一樣抓住鎳，透過後續運輸蛋白將其送進通往籽粒的管道。 研究發現，在生長點和籽粒中，鎳是關鍵零件：鎳不夠，尿素不能正常代謝，後續氨基酸、蛋白質形成就會受影響，從而影響玉米生長和種子萌發。這也意味著，對一些本身籽粒鎳含量偏低的玉米品種，或種植在鹼性土壤等容易缺鎳環境中的玉米，如果出現產量下降、種子發芽率低或萌發不齊等問題，或可透過補鎳來解決。 長期以來，學界認為禾本科植物鎳需求低、鎳缺乏罕見。這項研究首次提供了鎳作為作物生長必需元素的遺傳學證據，鑑定出禾本科植物控制鎳長距離運輸的基因，為透過營養調控和分子育種等方式提升禾本科作物產量與種子活力開闢了新方向。

### 2026-11-11 10:02:33  #宏觀 #國際

【中非標準化合作交流會在京舉辦釋出】11月9日，中非標準化合作交流會全體會議在北京舉辦，會議以標準“軟聯通”共促中非合作高質量發展為主題。市場監管總局副局長、國家標準委主任鄧志勇，國際標準化組織（ISO）主席哈立德·蘇菲出席並致辭。會議指出，當前中非合作已提升至新時代全天候命運共同體新階段，中非標準化合作務實推進、成果豐碩。中國已與埃及、摩洛哥、塞內加爾、尚比亞等國及非洲標準化組織（ARSO）等區域組織簽署標準化合作檔案，建成10萬畝農業標準化合作示範區，常態化中非標準資訊分享機制已覆蓋16個國家，累計收集標準題目9萬餘條、標準文字9000餘項。會議提出三點合作方向：一是以中非標準合作支援非洲現代化發展，圍繞環境保護、資源開發、基礎設施、數字智慧等重點方向推廣標準應用；二是以中非標準合作促進經貿往來、產業合作，加強農產品、機電產品、新能源汽車等重點產品標準協調，推進中非標準化合作與研究中心建設；三是以中非標準合作共同促進國際標準化治理，加強在ISO、國際電工委員會（IEC）框架下協調配合，聯合推動消除貧困、產供鏈合作、可再生能源等領域國際標準制定，讓中非標準合作成為標準促進全球可持續發展的生動樣板。會議釋出中非標準化合作與研究中心發展願景及重點合作計劃，交換多份雙邊合作檔案，正式上線中非標準資訊平臺，同步對外發布《中非標準化合作發展藍皮書》及合作實踐案例。

South Korean Shares Rise on Bargain Hunting — The benchmark KOSPI edged up 0.52% to around 5,266 on Monday, extending gains for another session as bargain hunting and strength in battery makers and financial firms supported the index. Sentiment also improved slightly following strong trade data, with South Korea's exports more than doubling year-on-year to $33.5 billion in the first 10 days of November, driven largely by a 331.2% surge in semiconductor shipments. Notable gainers included LG Energy Solution (+1.51%), Samsung SDI (+1.64%), KB Financial (+0.72%), and Shinhan Financial (+0.82%). Meanwhile, technology shares declined, with Samsung Electronics falling 0.96% and SK hynix shedding 0.99%, as hotter-than-expected US inflation fueled concerns that the Federal Reserve could maintain tighter monetary policy for longer. US consumer prices rose 0.3% month-on-month in October, above expectations, while Wall Street also closed lower overnight, with the Dow falling 0.3% and the Nasdaq shedding 0.74%.

### 2026-11-11 09:23:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Australia 10Y Yield Rises Past 5% — Australia's 10-year government bond yield held above 5%, after climbing off a recent ten-week low, tracking a broader selloff in global bond markets.US Treasuries led the move, with the 10-year yield rising past the 5% mark after a hotter-than-anticipated US inflation reading boosted chances for a Federal Reserve interest-rate hike of half ?a percentage point next month. The rise in US borrowing costs spilled over into Australian debt markets, pushing local yields higher. Meanwhile, a series of weak economic surveys pointed to a deteriorating outlook for the Australian economy. The disappointing readings reinforced expectations that the Reserve Bank of Australia has finished its tightening cycle following five interest rate increases this year, which brought the cash rate to an eighteen-year high of 4.85%. Markets now imply an 82% probability that rates have peaked, with a quarter-point rate cut fully priced in by the end of 2027.

Australian Shares Subdued — Australian equities were little changed in Wednesday morning trade, hovering near 9,133 as gains in utilities, industrial services, and consumer stocks were largely offset by losses in miners, commercial services, and transport. Traders digested fresh data showing Q3 housing lending fell to a five-quarter low amid elevated interest rates. Business sentiment also hit a six-month low in October, while consumer mood sank to a four-year low in November. Overnight, Wall Street ended mostly lower after hotter-than-expected U.S. inflation data lifted expectations of further Fed rate hikes. Regionally, China's trade surplus topped $1 trillion in the first 10 months of the year, supported by strong high-tech exports amid an AI infrastructure boom, which helped offset weak domestic demand. BHP Group slipped 0.3%, and Rio Tinto fell 0.8%. Macquarie Group (-1.5%) and Insurance Australia (-0.8%) also dropped. Conversely, Telix Pharmaceuticals (2.4%), Codan (2.2%), and PLS Group (1.4%) rose.

【東莞信託有限公司原董事張孟軍嚴重違紀違法被開除黨籍】東莞市紀委監委11月11日通報，東莞市紀委監委對東莞信託有限公司原董事張孟軍嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，張孟軍喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，廉潔底線失守，違規收受禮金，違規從事營利活動，為謀取不正當利益給予國家工作人員以財物；將公權力異化為謀取個人私利的工具，侵吞公共財物；搞權錢交易，利用職務便利為他人在辦理銀行貸款業務等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。張孟軍嚴重違反黨的廉潔紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌貪汙、受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》等有關規定，經東莞市紀委常委會會議研究，決定給予張孟軍開除黨籍處分；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌職務犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

Optimism towards AI stocks was further supported by reports that Anthropic could begin trading as early as next week, targeting a fundraising of up to $90 billion at a diluted valuation of approximately $1.4 trillion. Japanese equities also outperformed a weaker overnight session on Wall Street, where Nvidia’s losses and hotter-than-expected US inflation data pressured broader markets. Among the notable gainers were technology and AI-related stocks, with Advantest rising 0.7%, Taiyo Yuden climbing 3.6%, and Tokyo Electron advancing 1.1%. Financial and consumer shares also moved higher, as Mitsubishi UFJ gained 1.7% and Fast Retailing added 1.1%.

### 2026-11-11 08:59:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #other #currency #Importance 1

Bitcoin Hits 3-Month Low — Bitcoin fell below $73,500, reaching its lowest level since mid-August, as hotter-than-expected US inflation strengthened expectations of further interest rate hikes and weighed on demand for riskier assets. The decline brings its losses for the year to around 16%. US core consumer prices rose 0.3% month-on-month in October, exceeding market expectations, while headline inflation accelerated to 0.4%. Ahead of the inflation release, Fed Governor Christopher Waller had signaled that a 50 basis point rate increase in December should be considered if core inflation came in at 0.3% or higher. Meanwhile, Dallas Fed President Lorie Logan indicated after the release that a faster pace of rate hikes may be possible. Markets subsequently increased bets on further rate hikes through mid-2027. This drove Treasury yields and the US dollar higher, adding to pressure on cryptocurrencies.

今日12大單品的終端零售總價再度大幅下挫，洋河夢之藍M6+、古井貢古20、青花郎的跌幅都達到或超過6元，導致整體價盤賣壓沉重。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10479元，較昨日重跌28元，連續兩天創下今年9月中旬以來的近兩個月新低。

Australia Q3 Housing Lending Hits 5-Quarter Low — The value of new owner-occupier loan commitments for dwellings in Australia fell 5.3% quarter-on-quarter to AUD 56.45 billion in Q3 2026, the lowest in five quarters, deepening from a marginally revised 2.0% decline in the previous period. First-home buyer demand dropped further (-8.0% vs -5.4% in Q2), as did lending to non-first-home buyers (-3.5% vs -2.7%). On an annual basis, owner-occupier lending dipped 4.6%. Meanwhile, investment lending for housing tumbled 32.3% to AUD 24.71 billion, its lowest level in three years, much steeper than a 11.2% decline in Q2. On a yearly basis, investment loan values plunged 38.1%.

### 2026-11-11 08:40:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Foreign Direct Investment #Importance 1

Philippines FDI Rises 24.9% in August — Net foreign direct investment (FDI) inflows in the Philippines rose by 24.9% year-on-year to USD 690 million in August 2026. The increase was driven by higher net equity capital investments other than reinvestment of earnings and reinvestment of earnings, which grew by 29.5% and 50.2%, respectively, while net debt instrument investments declined by 16.7%. In the first eight months of the year, net FDI inflows decreased by 18.6% to USD 4.8 billion, from USD 5.9 billion in the same period last year, reflecting lower net investments in debt instruments (-30.0%), mainly due to reduced intercompany borrowings from foreign direct investors. Reinvestment of earnings also declined by 2.7%. Equity capital placements came primarily from Japan, the United States, and Singapore, with funds channeled largely into the manufacturing, financial and insurance, and real estate industries.

### 2026-11-11 08:38:49  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Aussie Dollar Slumps to 7-Month Low — The Australian dollar held below $0.69, settling at its lowest level since late March as weak domestic economic surveys and a stronger US dollar weighed on the currency. Consumer sentiment fell to a four-year low in November, while business conditions remained stuck in negative territory, a level not seen in six years, pointing to persistent weakness in the domestic economy. The disappointing readings reinforced expectations that the Reserve Bank of Australia has finished its tightening cycle following five interest rate increases this year, which brought the cash rate to an eighteen-year high of 4.85%. Markets now imply an 82% probability that rates have peaked, with a quarter-point rate cut fully priced in by the end of 2027. In contrast, hotter-than-expected US core inflation, which rose 0.3% in October, lifted expectations for a potential 50-basis-point rate hike by the Federal Reserve next month, supporting the US dollar and adding pressure on the Aussie.

【中信建投玻璃纖維行業2027年投資策略：新技術看好無布率先落地】中信建投證券釋出玻璃纖維2027年投資策略報告，看好LDK2代布成為特種布中未來潛在應用空間最大的品種。透過拆解各代NVDA AI伺服器PCB BOM，中信建投發現LDK2代布在平臺迭代中應用延續性與需求成長性兼具。Rubin及後續代際LDK2單GPU耗用量可提升至Blackwell的約2.8倍及以上。此外，看好LDK2代在ASIC伺服器中應用滲透率提升，1.6T交換機放量拉動其需求增長。經測算，LDK2代布2026—2028年供需缺口持續擴大，2028年缺口超0.9億米。預計2027年下半年國產織布機在普通布生產上批次應用。在豐田、津田駒等海外織布機優先供給特種布生產用的情況下，2027—2028年織布機供給瓶頸問題可由山東日發及浙江萬利等國產廠商實現有效緩解。新技術看好無布率先落地。三代布、有機布與無布三條新技術路線持續推進，其中無佈下遊應用場景最為明確，有望率先實現產業化落地。

### 2026-11-11 07:57:23  #央行 #國際

【美聯儲官員Paulson稱目前逐步收緊政策似乎是合適的】費城聯邦儲備銀行行長Anna Paulson表示，她贊成循序漸進地提高利率，並補充稱薪資壓力有限，通脹預期仍與央行2%的目標一致。Paulson週二在費城聯儲組織的一次活動上表示，“就目前而言，進一步逐步收緊政策似乎是合適的。”她補充說，“如果通脹預期上升、工資壓力增大或潛在通脹走高，我會希望更快地採取行動。但目前，我還沒有看到這種情況。”美聯儲官員上月將基準利率維持在3.75%至4%的區間不變，此前他們在9月加息0.25個百分點。自10月會議以來，多位政策制定者表示，需要進一步收緊政策才能將通脹率控制在2%的目標範圍內。Paulson表示，“一次會議不行動，那只是節奏問題，而不是方向的問題。可能還有更多工作要做。但我傾向於在條件允許的情況下逐步收緊政策。”她補充說，放慢節奏可以讓家庭、企業和金融部門有時間進行調整，同時也能讓政策制定者有時間評估資料。她表示，“當政策方向明確時，循序漸進的方式效果最佳。如果市場預期政策將進一步收緊，那麼循序漸進的做法就不會讓我們落後於應有的目標。”

Brent Hovers Near 10-Week Lows — Brent crude held below $92 per barrel on Wednesday, hovering close to ten-week lows on signs of easing tensions between the US and Iran. Meanwhile, Congressional Democrats are considering leveraging the upcoming government funding deadline to push the Trump administration toward ending the conflict. However, uncertainty persists as Iran demands relief from US sanctions and the naval blockade before agreeing to peace talks. At the same time, Middle Eastern oil shipments have nearly returned to prewar levels despite ongoing attacks on vessels in the Strait of Hormuz. The international oil benchmark has fallen more than 20% from its late-October peak, reversing much of its recent gains as hopes for de-escalation strengthened and regional oil exports continued to recover.

### 2026-11-11 07:54:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Senegal #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Senegal Inflation Rate Hits 10-Month High — Senegal’s annual inflation rate accelerated to 1.6% in October 2026 from 1.0% in the previous month. It was the highest inflation rate since December 2025, as food and beverage inflation, the largest component of the inflation basket, accelerated to 2.1% from 0.9% in September, marking the fastest increase since December 2025. Inflation also accelerated in housing and utilities (0.6% vs 0.1% in September), restaurants and hotels (2.1% vs 1.8%), recreation, sports and culture (1.2% vs 0.8%), education (2.5% vs 1.0%), and clothing and footwear (1.4% vs 1.1%). Meanwhile, inflation moderated in transport (0.9% vs 1.2%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (9.0% vs 12.0%), and financial services (0.3% vs 1.0%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.4%, easing from a 1.1% gain in the prior month and marking the softest increase in four months.

【美國住房與城市發展部收緊移民家庭住房援助規定】美國住房與城市發展部（HUD）於週二敲定了一項備受爭議的規則，要求對獲得住房援助的家庭進行公民身份核查，此舉可能導致數萬人被迫搬離。該規定被稱為“混合身份家庭規則”（RIN 2501-AE16）。新規要求，所有申請住房專案的人員，無論年齡大小，都必須接受公民身份或移民身份核查。此外，在所有家庭成員的身份核實完畢之前，該家庭僅能獲得臨時性的按比例分配的住房援助。HUD部長斯科特·特納（Scott Turner）在一份宣告中表示：“美國的無家可歸退伍軍人、殘疾人、老年人和家庭暴力受害者苦苦排在等候名單上，而非法移民和不符合資格的人卻在騙取我們的系統資源，這是不可接受的。美國納稅人將不再為此買單。承諾已定，說到做到。”根據之前的規定，家庭可以根據家庭成員中公民或符合條件的移民人數按比例獲得租金援助。但現行法律不允許無證移民獲得住房援助。

### 2026-11-11 07:34:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #El Salvador #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

El Salvador Inflation Rate Highest Since 2023 — Annual inflation in El Salvador accelerated to 3.91% in October 2026 from 3.60% in September. It was the highest inflation rate since May 2023, with food and non-alcoholic beverage prices rising at the fastest pace since October 2023 (5.06% vs. 4.44% in September). Inflation also accelerated in the transport sector (10.64% vs 2.89%), restaurants and hotels (4.61% vs 0.79%), miscellaneous goods and services (2.26% vs 2.23%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (3.04% vs 2.50%), and health (2.76% vs 0.23%), while rebounding in housing and utilities (2.14% vs -0.64%) and recreation and culture (3.20% vs -0.93%). Meanwhile, inflation eased in furniture and household goods (0.27% vs 2.12%) and education (0.79% vs 3.50%). By contrast, consumer prices fell in communications (-0.70% vs 9.63%) and clothing and footwear (-1.05% vs 2.50%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.58%, following a 0.45% gain in September, marking the fastest increase in six months.

【OpenAI營收增至100億美元但持續面臨鉅額虧損】從第二季度到第三季度，OpenAI的營收增長了約50%至100億美元，但該公司仍報告了鉅額運營虧損，這表明其在追趕行業領先者Anthropic的努力中呈現出喜憂參半的結果。週二，該公司向投資者公佈了最新業績，而Anthropic正準備於下週進行備受期待的IPO。Claude的開發商Anthropic近期披露，其第三季度營收已增至170億美元，且該季度的運營虧損縮小至5.67億美元。據媒體查閱的檔案顯示，OpenAI向投資者表示，從第二季度到第三季度，其調整後運營利潤率從-150%改善至-109%。儘管如此，根據這些資料，該公司的運營虧損仍從101億美元小幅上升至109億美元。在利潤豐厚的AI產品企業市場中，OpenAI正加速追趕Anthropic，並降低了其最新模型的定價以擴大市場份額。該公司最近任命了新的首席營收官Dali Rajic，專注於這一業務板塊。目前，企業市場在OpenAI整體銷售額中所佔的比例正穩步上升。

### 2026-11-11 07:14:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Unemployment Rate #Importance 2

South Korea Jobless Rate Lowest in 25 Months — South Korea’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate fell to 2.5% in October 2026 from 2.6% in the previous month, reaching its lowest level since September 2024. The economy added 139,000 jobs, exceeding the 100,000 mark for the fourth consecutive month and bringing total employment to 29.18 million. Healthcare and social welfare led job creation, adding 249,000 workers, followed by transportation and storage (+48,000) and business facilities management and support services (+37,000). In contrast, accommodation and food services employment fell by 137,000, while agriculture, forestry and fishing (-101,000) and construction (-42,000) also recorded declines. Manufacturing employment slipped by 7,000, reversing the previous month's increase. Meanwhile, the number of unemployed people declined by 56,000 year-on-year to 602,000, while the economically inactive population increased by 179,000 to 16.30 million. Youth employment for those aged 15 to 29 fell by 1.7 percentage points to 42.9%.

### 2026-11-11 07:07:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Reuters Tankan Index #Importance 2

Japan Manufacturers' Mood Highest in Near 5 Years — The Reuters Tankan index for Japanese manufacturers rose for the fourth consecutive month to +27 in November 2026, from +22 in the previous month, marking its highest reading since August 2021, supported by continued strength in semiconductor and data center demand. Electronics surged to +39 from +25, with precision machinery, including chip equipment, leading the gains by climbing to +42 from +38, boosted by strong demand from both domestic and overseas markets. Other sectors also reported gains, with chemicals rising to +33 from +27, while oil refining and ceramics advanced to +25 from +14. Meanwhile, non-manufacturers’ sentiment increased to +35, the highest since June 2018, from +23, which was the lowest reading since November 2024, reflecting a sharp rebound in confidence in the service and domestic sectors. Looking three months ahead, manufacturers expect sentiment to improve to +28, while non-manufacturers expect sentiment to weaken to +29.

### 2026-11-11 07:04:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #API Crude Oil Stock Change #Importance 1

US Crude Inventories Surge Unexpectedly — US crude oil inventories rose by 9.0 million barrels in the week ended November 6, defying market forecasts for a 1 million-barrel decline and reversing a 5.267 million-barrel drop in the previous week. The latest result marked the first increase in six weeks. Meanwhile, gasoline inventories increased by just 4,000 barrels, slowing sharply from a 1.427 million-barrel build previously, while distillate stocks rose by 1.5 million barrels after falling by 270,000 barrels in the prior week. At Cushing, inventories increased by 182,000 barrels, following a 511,000-barrel build in the previous week. Separately, 2.7 million barrels of crude were released from the Strategic Petroleum Reserve (SPR) to support commercial inventories, bringing total SPR stocks down to 279.5 million barrels. US crude oil production reached a record 14.003 million barrels per day in the week ended October 30, up from 13.992 million bpd a week earlier.

【韓正會見聯合國秘書長古特雷斯】當地時間11月10日，習近平主席特別代表、國家副主席韓正在土耳其安塔利亞出席《聯合國氣候變化框架公約》第三十一次締約方大會（COP31）領導人峰會期間會見聯合國秘書長古特雷斯。韓正轉達習近平主席對古特雷斯的親切問候。韓正表示，中國高度重視氣候變化問題。去年，習近平主席宣佈了中國2035年國家自主貢獻，明確提出“力爭做得更好”，體現了中國應對氣候變化的堅定決心和最大努力。今年中國又提出2030年非化石能源發電量佔比達到50%、電能佔終端能源消費比重達到35%等目標。中方聚焦目標制定和行動落實，強調言必信、行必果，將持續為全球氣候治理提供寶貴的穩定性和確定性。韓正表示，中國同聯合國一道開展應對氣候變化國際合作，堅定維護、全面落實《聯合國氣候變化框架公約》及其《巴黎協定》，大力開展應對氣候變化南南合作，積極響應全民早期預警倡議。當前全球應對氣候變化面臨諸多挑戰，特別是發展中國家落實國家自主貢獻面臨資金短缺、技術落後和能力不足等困難，需國際社會共同解決。中方期待COP31在秘書長先生的領導下取得新的進展和共識。(新華社)

NZX 50 Retreats from Record High on Profit-Taking — The NZX 50 shed 70 points, or 0.5%, to 14,097 in Wednesday morning trade, halting the previous session's gains as traders took profit after the index hit a record high the day before. The broader index followed a decline on Wall Street overnight as inflation came in hotter than expected. Traders continued to monitor coalition talks, while Labour market leaders identified National's Simeon Brown and Labour's Barbara Edmonds as the leading candidates for Finance Minister. The names emerged after current Finance Minister Nicola Willis failed to secure a seat in Parliament. Meanwhile, easing oil prices limited the decline as inflation concerns and expectations of interest rate hikes eased. Financials, real estate, and industrials mainly weighed on the index, with early losers including Fletcher Building (-2.0%), Infratil (-1.6%), Westpac Banking Corp. (-1.3%), Auckland International Airport (-0.9%), and Summerset Group (-0.9%).

【泰國內閣批准資料中心七級監管政策】泰國內閣10日批准資料中心監管政策，決定按用電規模實施七級分類管理，20兆瓦及以上專案將面臨更嚴格的選址要求。泰國副總理兼財政部長艾尼迪表示，政府將設立一站式服務中心，統籌資料中心專案審批。據泰國國家經濟和社會發展委員會秘書長丹努差介紹，資料中心將按照用電規模劃分為七級：低於0.5兆瓦、0.5至5兆瓦、5至10兆瓦、10至20兆瓦、20至50兆瓦、50至200兆瓦以及200兆瓦及以上。其中，20兆瓦以下專案可設在商業區域，20兆瓦及以上專案則須設在工業區域。根據新政策，泰國政府還將建立資料中心一站式服務中心，由泰國數字經濟與社會部負責推進，統籌專案篩選、審批及相關資訊管理，並透過資料中心資訊平臺彙總專案資料。(中新聞)

【CoreWeave Q3 revenue tops estimates; debt burden remains key risk】CoreWeave reported Q3 revenue up 167% YoY to $3.66bn vs. $3.55bn expected; adjusted loss per share $1.27 vs. $1.46 expected. The company raised full-year revenue guidance to $13.0–13.3bn from $12.4–13.2bn. Shares jumped as much as 8.7% in after-hours trade, later trimming gains to about 2%; the stock fell >40% over the past month and is down 32% YTD. CoreWeave faces heavy leverage — debt exceeded $50bn at end-June and its 2030 notes yield roughly 15.2%. Management says capex is tied to signed customer contracts and supported by customer prepayments, and that current backlog can cover debt obligations. Microsoft has said some emerging cloud providers’ data center projects are delayed and under tighter oversight; several officials elected in the US midterms have signaled intentions to slow or block data center builds, raising sector expansion uncertainty. CoreWeave plans to broaden revenue via new services and Asia expansion.

Equinox Gold tumbled 6.2% as adjusted Q3 EPS fell short of expectations, and Endeavour Silver lost 2.8% after narrowing its full-year gold production guidance. Meanwhile, oil prices extended losses as investors increasingly anticipated a diplomatic resolution to the US-Iran conflict. Lower energy prices provided some relief to credit-sensitive sectors: Great-West Lifeco rose 2.7% and Fairfax Financial added 2.5%. iA Financial rose 1.5% after beating EPS estimates and raising its quarterly common dividend. Still, most major banks edged lower. Elsewhere, Hydro One gained 0.6% despite missing EPS expectations.

### 2026-11-11 05:13:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stock Market Closes Lower Following CPI Report — US stock indices closed lower on Tuesday as higher-than-expected inflation weighed on stocks and bonds following hawkish signals from Fed officials. The S&P 500 lost 0.4%, the Dow shed 158 points, and the Nasdaq declined 0.6%. A surprisingly high CPI reinforced expectations of a quarter-point December hike, with money markets pricing some chance of an even larger move and further hikes next year. Meanwhile, Anthropic is expected to begin trading as early as next week, seeking to raise up to $90 billion at a diluted valuation of around $1.4 trillion. The offering comes amid strong demand for AI services, though growing concerns over excessive capital expenditure have raised questions about the sustainability of the sector’s investment boom. Chipmakers were mostly lower, with Nvidia falling 5.5%, extending the previous session’s losses, while Micron shed 1.1%. Hyperscalers also declined, with Microsoft losing 2.2% and Oracle retreating 1.5%.

### 2026-11-11 05:06:24  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500

【Fed Governor Bowman: Inflation still needs to be controlled; weigh costs of further tightening】Michelle Bowman, Fed vice chair for supervision, said inflationary pressures persist while the labor market is stabilizing and inflation still has room to fall. She expects October core PCE YoY to rise to 3.3%, driven mainly by energy-price increases and AI-related demand, and said the lack of progress on inflation is not due to an overheated labor market. Bowman said core PCE could move toward 2% next year as AI-related construction demand cools and Middle East conflict pressures ease. She warned recent equity declines, higher Treasury yields and a stronger dollar have already tightened financial conditions, and the economy has strengthened year-to-date. Any further policy tightening must be weighed against costs to interest-rate-sensitive sectors; persistence of AI demand and the path of geopolitical risk will be key near-term determinants of policy. She did not explicitly call for rate hikes but stressed the Fed must still drive inflation back to 2%.

### 2026-11-11 04:06:59  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #S&P500 #Impact mixed #Nasdaq100 #WTI

【FITCH: Democratic midterm wins have mixed impact on US AI, energy and healthcare sectors】FITCH says Democratic midterm victories would have uneven effects on US corporate credit; most sectors’ cash flows are expected to remain stable. Tighter approvals and higher costs in key data‑center states (Texas, Georgia, Ohio) could slow AI infrastructure investment and support the view that AI capex may peak around 2027; a construction slowdown would also ease longer‑term overcapacity risk. In energy, oil & gas firms may face stricter drilling and environmental reviews; power and industrials could see weaker demand from data‑center pullback, though grid upgrades and broader infrastructure spending provide offsetting support. Healthcare policy is unlikely to change materially in the near term; defense firms benefit from record order backlogs and bipartisan support, so major policy shifts are not expected.

### 2026-11-11 03:20:52  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Fall as Hot Inflation Fuels Fed Rate Hike Bets — US equities fell on Tuesday as higher-than-expected inflation weighed on stocks and bonds, amid hawkish signals from Fed officials and investor scrutiny of the upcoming Anthropic IPO. The S&P 500 lost 0.5%, the Dow shed 200 points and the Nasdaq declined 1%. Both headline and core US CPI rose more than expected in October, reinforcing expectations of a Fed rate hike next month and lifting bets on multiple rate increases next year. Meanwhile, Anthropic is expected to begin trading as early as next week, seeking to raise up to $90 billion at a diluted valuation of around $1.4 trillion. The offering comes amid strong demand for AI services, although growing concerns over excessive capital expenditure have raised questions about the sustainability of the sector's investment boom. Chipmakers were mostly lower, with Nvidia falling nearly 6%, extending the previous session's losses, while Micron shed almost 3%. Hyperscalers also declined, with Microsoft and Oracle retreating more than 2%.

### 2026-11-11 02:56:48  #MKT News #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【NYT: Syria weighs sending thousands of troops to Saudi Arabia, Yemen to counter Houthi forces】The New York Times reports Syria is weighing deployment of several thousand troops to Saudi Arabia and Yemen to assist Saudi efforts against Iran-backed Houthi forces. Syrian and Yemeni officials said Syrian military reconnaissance teams have in recent weeks surveyed deployment sites and coordinated with local forces; about 150 Syrian soldiers and commanders travelled last month with Saudi military personnel to Tamud in northeast Yemen near the Saudi border. Damascus has made no final decision. Any deployment would likely operate under the Mecca Joint Defense Agreement signed in August by Saudi Arabia, Turkey and Pakistan, with an ostensible mission to protect the Saudi border and prevent Houthi ground infiltration. Riyadh remains a major donor and investor in Syria; last week Saudi and Syrian authorities signed reconstruction and emergency-services deals worth over $400m. Syria’s military remains in post-war reorganisation and faces Israeli strikes and domestic extremist threats, so an overseas deployment could add to internal security pressures.

Corn Rebounds form 3-Month Low After WASDE — Corn futures rebounded to $4.50 per bushel from a three-month low of $4.40 on November 9th after the USDA's WASDE report downwardly revised the supply backdrop in the US. US corn stocks at the end of the marketing year are expected to be 1.758 billion bushels, revised lower from last month's projection of 1.849 billion, consistent with a reduction in annual production to 15.89 billion bushels from 16.03 billion. Meanwhile, the US harvest reached 81% completion as of November 8th, five percentage points behind the five-year average, with wet conditions slowing fieldwork in key producing states such as Iowa. The data trimmed the recent decline in corn prices, which had been partially driven by the slump in fuel costs amid fresh optimism over diplomacy in Iran, limiting demand for ethanol.

### 2026-11-11 02:07:53  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Copper Falls to 4-Month Low — Copper fell to $6.20 per pound in November, the lowest in four months, tracking base metals with exposure to data centers and electrification projects as markets reconsidered the magnitude of demand growth for the sector's infrastructure buildout. Prices erased their momentary rebound as hotter-than-expected inflation in the US drove markets to price in multiple Federal Reserve rate hikes in the coming quarters, lifting the dollar and borrowing costs for debt-ridden AI infrastructure companies. The data compounded recent pressure from the Democratic Party assuming a majority in the US Congress, which is associated with policies that may slow investment in infrastructure projects that require large amounts of copper. A Democratic Congress also reduced bids for copper futures in the US as tariffs on the metal threatened by President-elect Trump became less likely to be implemented.

### 2026-11-11 02:07:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 1

Dollar Extends Gains on Hotter CPI, Hawkish Fed Comments — The dollar index extended its gains to 102.3 on Tuesday, amid growing expectations that the Fed could pursue a more aggressive pace of interest rate hikes in the coming months, following a hotter-than-expected inflation report and increasingly hawkish comments from Fed officials. Consumer prices rose 0.4% in October, exceeding market expectations of a 0.3% increase, while core CPI advanced 0.3% for the third consecutive month, also above forecasts of 0.2%. Adding to the hawkish outlook, Fed Governor Waller warned that a 50bps rate hike in December may be necessary. Meanwhile, Dallas Fed President Logan supported at least two additional 25bps increases, suggesting that further tightening may be required at a faster pace than policymakers have pursued this autumn. Markets currently assign an approximately 88% probability to a 25bps rate hike at the Fed's next meeting, while further increases in January and potentially April are also being priced in.

### 2026-11-11 02:07:32  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cotton Falls From 2-Week High — Cotton futures fell below 79 cents per pound after reaching its highest level in two weeks, as traders weighed a lower global supply outlook and signs of improving export demand against an accelerating US harvest. A quickly progressing US harvest continues to weigh on prices, with 71% of the crop harvested by the week ended November 8th, above the five-year average of 63%. Providing an offsetting pressure, in its latest WASDE report, the USDA cut its 2026/27 global production forecast to 25.58 million tonnes from 25.68 million, while leaving consumption unchanged. Support also came from USDA's weekly export sales report for the week ending October 29th that showed an increase of 28% from the previous week, lending further support to the market. However, gains remain limited by intensifying competition from Brazil, whose exports rose 15.1% year-on-year in October, highlighting the country's growing influence in global trade.

Wheat Holds Near 4-Month Low — Wheat remained below $6 a bushel, hovering near their lowest level since early July, as traders awaited the outcome of US-brokered peace talks between Russia and Ukraine. Officials from the two countries met in Abu Dhabi for their first such discussions since February, raising hopes that a de-escalation could eventually restore Black Sea grain trade. Meanwhile, expectations of stronger demand for French supplies are growing as buyers seek alternatives to restricted Russian shipments. Lithuania also announced that Russian and Belarusian grain arriving through the port of Klaipeda would face origin checks, potentially delaying exports for months. Nevertheless, broader market sentiment remained pressured by expectations of abundant global supplies, with larger harvests forecast in Australia and Argentina. Wheat prices have fallen steadily from their August 28 peak of $7.67 a bushel, a 3-1/2-year high, as global production is expected to reach its second-highest level on record.

Ibovespa Slips on Copom Minutes — The Ibovespa fell nearly 0.5% to around 171,500 on Tuesday, erasing earlier gains as market focus shifted to the Copom minutes and prospects of a US rate hike. Copom’s latest minutes showed that moderation in activity may intensify, but deteriorating inflation expectations and rising consumer costs require monetary policy to remain restrictive. Meanwhile, hotter-than-expected October US inflation reinforced expectations of further Federal Reserve hikes. Credit-sensitive stocks posted losses, with major banks and utilities down: Itaú shed nearly 1%, Bradesco lost over 1.5%, and Axia dropped more than 1%. Vale pared recent gains, falling 2% amid lower iron ore prices. On the earnings front, Embraer soared nearly 9% after reporting revenue above estimates and setting a Q3 record. Soybeans Rebound from 3-Month Low — Soybean futures surged 3% above $12 a bushel, recovering from three-month lows and tracking gains across grain markets after the latest WASDE report. The USDA raised its estimate of US soybean production to 4.574 billion bushels, with a record average yield of 53.3 bushels per acre, up from its October forecast of 4.562 billion bushels and 53.1 bushels per acre. In contrast, the agency cut its corn production estimate to 15.893 billion bushels, below both its previous forecast and analysts’ expectations, citing a lower projected yield. As of November 8, the US soybean harvest was 92% complete and corn harvesting had reached 81%. Expectations of large crops in Brazil could limit further gains, although renewed optimism over Chinese demand provided support. Beijing has pledged to purchase 25 million metric tons of US soybeans annually for three years, alongside additional agricultural products, raising hopes for stronger exports ahead of a possible Trump-Xi meeting later this month.

Palladium Stays Near 3-Week High — Palladium futures traded around $1,000 an ounce, near a three-week high, as traders assess a subdued demand outlook against diplomatic efforts in the Middle East. A Democratic sweep in the US midterms could complicate President Trump’s foreign policy agenda and efforts to secure additional funds for the war with Iran, offering some relief to inflationary concerns. However, stronger-than-expected US inflation and labour market data raised bets of a Fed rate hike next month, pushing borrowing costs higher and limiting demand for the non-yielding metal. Over the longer term, elevated financing costs, alongside weaker vehicle production and the gradual shift towards EVs, weigh on automotive demand, a major source of palladium consumption. Still, a modest recovery is expected in 2027 amid stable hybrid demand. Constrained output from Russia, the world's largest palladium producer, offers some support, with Nornickel warning that an overhaul at one of its plants could extend into 2028.

### 2026-11-11 00:51:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

European Natural Gas Prices Rise from 1-Month Low — European natural gas prices rose above €85/MWh, up from a one-month low of €82.5 hit on November 9, as concerns over tight winter supplies outweighed optimism about a potential resolution to the US-Iran conflict. Europe’s already low storage levels are declining rapidly amid colder weather, lower wind generation and increased heating demand. Net withdrawals last week rose nearly sixfold from the previous week, while EU storage stood below 73%, well under the five-year seasonal average of 89%; Germany’s reserves were below 60%. Supply pressures have been compounded by disruptions to Middle Eastern LNG exports, constrained Norwegian pipeline flows due to outages and intensifying competition from Asian buyers. However, hopes for de-escalation have improved after US President Donald Trump signaled that further strikes on Iran might not be necessary. Meanwhile, Qatar’s LNG loadings picked up in recent weeks, suggesting efforts to restore exports despite disruption around the Strait.

CAC 40 Hits Two-Month High — The CAC 40 rose 0.6% to close at 8,279 on Tuesday, its highest level since early September, extending gains from the previous session as oil prices fell and AI-related stocks rebounded. Brent crude dropped below $90 for the first time since August after President Trump said the Iran war could end fairly soon and played down new strikes. Legrand rose 1.2% and STMicroelectronics 0.9% as AI-related stocks rebounded, while Safran jumped 2.4%, Thales added 1.9% and Airbus gained 1.7%. Banks also advanced, with Société Générale up 2.5%, BNP Paribas 2.2% and AXA 2.0%. In contrast, EssilorLuxottica fell 1.4%, Stellantis 3.4% and Dassault Systèmes 1.6%, while TotalEnergies lost 0.8% as oil prices declined. ### 2026-11-11 00:33:53  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Sugar Futures at Near 2-Month Low — US sugar futures eased to near 18 cents per pound, their lowest level since mid-September, pressured by prospects of increased supply from India. Recent data from the Indian Sugar and Bioenergy Manufacturers Association (ISMA) showed that the country produced 1.29 million tons of sugar between October 1 and November 5, almost four times the 320,000 tons recorded in the same period last season. Meanwhile, the ongoing weakness of the Brazilian real is enhancing the competitiveness of Brazilian exports, while lower oil prices are reducing the relative attractiveness of ethanol production. However, persistent El Niño-related rains in Brazil's Center-South have hampered sugarcane harvesting and milling. The weather phenomenon appears to be affecting the region earlier than usual, with excessive rainfall complicating production prospects and raising uncertainty over the start of the next harvest. Overall, global supply risks remain elevated, with a potential deficit looming in 2026/27.

### 2026-11-11 00:25:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Platinum Falls From 3-Week High — Platinum futures fell below $1,570 an ounce, easing from a three-week high as growing expectations of another Fed rate hike dimmed the outlook for investment demand in the metal. Stronger-than-expected US inflation and labour market data boosted expectations of a rate increase next month, pushing borrowing costs higher. Higher rates put downward pressure on investment demand for the non-yielding metal. At the same time, elevated financing costs weigh on automotive demand, a major source of platinum consumption. The sector also faces headwinds from weaker vehicle production and the gradual shift towards EVs. Against this backdrop, expectations of a modest market surplus are adding to bearish pressures. However, rising hybrid production and continued demand for catalytic converters are helping cushion the decline, while longer-term demand from green hydrogen and industrial applications provides some support.

【葉門胡塞武裝稱打擊沙特及葉門境內多個目標】薩那訊息：葉門胡塞武裝10日稱，該組織對沙烏地阿拉伯及葉門境內多個目標發動了打擊。胡塞武裝軍事發言人葉海亞·薩雷亞當天透過該組織控制的馬西拉電視臺發表宣告說，胡塞武裝使用彈道導彈和無人機打擊了位於沙特首都利雅得的哈立德國王國際機場和利雅得的一個金融中心、沙特東西向輸油管道沿線泵站、沙特阿美石油公司設施以及葉門境內與沙特有關聯的部隊集結地。薩雷亞稱，這些行動是對沙特近期空襲葉門薩那省一居民區及塔伊茲省一市場的回應。薩雷亞在宣告中還要求外國企業和投資者撤出在沙特的投資，稱在沙特停止對葉門胡塞武裝控制區的“侵略和封鎖”之前不具備安全的投資環境。近期，沙特與胡塞武裝互襲力度增強。與此同時，胡塞武裝與沙特支援的葉門政府軍在多條戰線的交火也日趨激烈。(新華社)

Rice Futures Hover Around 2024-Highs — Rice prices eased to around $17.2 per hundredweight amid profit-taking, but remained close to their highest level since June 2024. Concerns over global supply persist, with El Niño-related weather disruptions threatening production across major rice-growing countries in Asia, including India, Thailand, Vietnam and Indonesia. India is particularly exposed, as El Niño contributed to its weakest monsoon in more than a decade. As a result, India's rice production is expected to decline for the first time in a decade and marking its steepest annual drop in nearly 20 years. However, the impact is likely to be cushioned by substantial inventories accumulated from previous record harvests. Thailand is also facing weather-related production risks, as El Niño threatens to bring hotter and drier conditions that could constrain irrigation water supplies and affect crop development. Meanwhile, flooding in parts of the country has disrupted agricultural activity and transportation.

US Household Debt Hits Record $19.1 Trillion — Total household debt in the US expanded by $334 billion from the prior quarter to $19.1 trillion in Q3 2026, the highest on record. Mortgage balances rose by $257 billion to $13.4 trillion, and home equity lines of credit (HELOC) balances rose by $23 billion to $481 billion, the largest increase since 2005. Credit card balances rose by $22 billion to $1.29 trillion, and auto loan balances increased by $21 billion to $1.73 trillion. Student loan balances edged up by $5 billion to $1.66 trillion. “Household debt levels rose moderately, led by increases in mortgage and home equity line of credit originations” said Donghoon Lee, Economic Research Advisor at the New York Fed. “Although delinquency transition rates for housing debts have crept up, the overall credit quality of these loans remains strong.”

Mexican Peso Slips on Narrowing US-Mexico Rate Spread Bets — The Mexican peso weakened to near 18.7 per USD from the three-week high of 18.5 touched on November 9th amid prospects of a narrower US-Mexico interest-rate differential. Hotter-than-expected US inflation in October reinforced expectations of further Federal Reserve hikes. A narrower rate differential would pressure the peso by reducing carry trade. Still, recent data limits the room for looser policy by Banxico. Annual inflation rose to 3.75% from 3.45% in September, above forecasts of 3.65%, moving further above Banxico’s 3% target. Core inflation edged up to 3.80% from 3.75%, broadly in line with forecasts of 3.78%. The central bank unexpectedly cut its benchmark rate to 6.25% at its latest meeting and revised forward guidance, signaling rates would remain at the new level for an “extended period.” Meanwhile, the Democratic Party’s victory in the US midterm elections could soften trade pressures on Mexican growth.

Norway Consumer Confidence Falls in Q4 — Norway’s adjusted consumer confidence indicator fell to -22.5 in the fourth quarter of 2026, down from a revised -19.1 in the previous quarter, signalling a further deterioration in household sentiment. The expectations barometer points to a marked shift in consumers’ outlook for their personal finances, with pessimism increasingly replacing optimism. While a majority of households previously expected their financial situation to improve over the coming year, the latest survey shows that more now anticipate a deterioration. The rise in pessimism is broad-based, spanning all demographic groups, including gender, age, education, income and geographical location. Notably, households in age groups traditionally carrying the highest levels of debt are among the most pessimistic.

Swiss Bond Yields Rise From 2-Month Low — The Swiss 10-year government bond yield rose above 0.45%, recovering from its lowest level in two months and tracking US Treasury yields higher as stronger-than-expected US inflation lifted expectations of another Fed rate hike. Although inflation reached a three-year high in October amid a weaker franc and rising energy costs, energy accounts for only a small share of the Swiss CPI basket. A decline in oil prices amid expectations of a diplomatic resolution to the conflict in the Middle East has also offered some relief. Policymakers in the BCB suggested that the elevated interest rates have already dampened economic activity in its latest meeting minutes, hinting that rate cuts may be necessary to tame slower growth and threats to unemployment.
